# Endpoint catalog

Generated by `scripts/gen_endpoint_catalog.py` from `endpoint_policy_seed.json`. Do not edit by hand: change the
seed, then run the script. How to use this table: `BRIEF.md` in this folder.

Source: Roblox `content/en-us/reference/cloud/openapi.json` at commit `710b20a5f4f1` of https://github.com/Roblox/creator-docs (2026-10-16). 992 operations on 35 hosts.

Verdicts: **BLOCK** = refused before Roblox, never forwarded. TOKEN = forwarded with Roxy's credential. ANON = forwarded, never with the credential. Roblox sign-in column: what Roblox's reference says the endpoint accepts (`cookie-required`, `cookie-optional`, `none`, `apikey/oauth-only`). Risk: 1 harmless, 2 low, 3 meaningful, 4 critical (what happens if a stranger could call it with Roxy's account).

Totals: BLOCK 779, TOKEN 161, ANON 52.

## Hosts Roxy allows that Roblox does not document

| Host | Default | Category | Risk | Why |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| billing.roblox.com | **BLOCK** | economy-spend | 4 | Not in the Roblox published reference. Payments, Robux purchases and payment methods of the signed-in account. Block the whole host. |
| engagementpayouts.roblox.com | **BLOCK** | creator-private-data | 3 | Not in the Roblox published reference. Premium engagement payout figures for universes the signed-in account owns. Block the whole host. |
| gamejoin.roblox.com | **BLOCK** | login-session-security | 4 | Not in the Roblox published reference. With a cookie it issues game join tickets for the signed-in account, so a caller could join servers as the Roxy account. Block the whole host. |
| search.roblox.com | ANON | public-read | 2 | Not in the Roblox published reference. Search results can be personalized for a signed-in viewer, so forward without the cookie. Review with real traffic. |
| translations.roblox.com | ANON | public-read | 1 | Not in the Roblox published reference. Website translation string bundles; the cookie adds nothing. Forward without it. |
| voice.roblox.com | **BLOCK** | account-settings-write | 3 | Not in the Roblox published reference. Voice chat eligibility, opt-in and settings of the signed-in account. Block the whole host. |

## ads.roblox.com

| Method | Path | Roblox sign-in | Verdict | Risk | Category | What it does and why | Docs |

| POST | `/v1/ads-tracking/click` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 2 | ads | Records an ad click as Roxy's account. ## assetdelivery.roblox.com

| Method | Path | Roblox sign-in | Verdict | Risk | Category | What it does and why | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/v1/asset` | cookie-optional | ANON | 2 | public-read | Downloads an asset's file by id, version, hash or userAssetId query params. Heavily used for public assets, but with Roxy's cookie it would also hand out private or copylocked assets the account is allowed to access, so forward without the cookie. *Note:* Permissions leak: with the cookie, Roblox serves any asset Roxy's account can access (its own or group-owned private models, audio, packages, copylocked places it can edit, assets shared to it through asset permissions). Strip the cookie and any caller-sent Roblox-Place-Id/permissionContext style headers should not grant extra access. If Roblox starts refusing anonymous downloads (401), only flip to ALLOW_TOKEN after confirming the account owns, edits and is shared on nothing private. Responses are redirects/locations to signed CDN URLs that expire; cache per asset id plus version for a short time only. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#assetdelivery_get_v1_asset) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/assetdelivery#assetdelivery_get_v1_asset) |
| GET | `/v1/assetId/{assetId}` | cookie-optional | ANON | 2 | public-read | Downloads an asset's file by asset id. Heavily used for public assets, but with Roxy's cookie it would also hand out private or copylocked assets the account is allowed to access, so forward without the cookie. *Note:* Permissions leak: with the cookie, Roblox serves any asset Roxy's account can access (its own or group-owned private models, audio, packages, copylocked places it can edit, assets shared to it through asset permissions). Strip the cookie and any caller-sent Roblox-Place-Id/permissionContext style headers should not grant extra access. If Roblox starts refusing anonymous downloads (401), only flip to ALLOW_TOKEN after confirming the account owns, edits and is shared on nothing private. Responses are redirects/locations to signed CDN URLs that expire; cache per asset id plus version for a short time only. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#assetdelivery_get_v1_assetId__assetId_) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/assetdelivery#assetdelivery_get_v1_assetId__assetId_) |

Same permissions leak as the single-asset routes, so forward without the cookie. *Note:* Permissions leak: with the cookie, Roblox serves any asset Roxy's account can access (its own or group-owned private models, audio, packages, copylocked places it can edit, assets shared to it through asset permissions). Strip the cookie and any caller-sent Roblox-Place-Id/permissionContext style headers should not grant extra access. If Roblox starts refusing anonymous downloads (401), only flip to ALLOW_TOKEN after confirming the account owns, edits and is shared on nothing private. Responses are redirects/locations to signed CDN URLs that expire; cache per asset id plus version for a short time only. The body items can carry their own access context fields; they are only safe without the cookie. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#assetdelivery_post_v1_assets_batch) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/assetdelivery#assetdelivery_post_v1_assets_batch) |

The body items can carry their own access context fields; they are only safe without the cookie. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#assetdelivery_post_v2_assets_batch) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/assetdelivery#assetdelivery_post_v2_assets_batch) |
| GET | `/v2/marAssetHash/{marAssetHash}/marCheckSum/{marCheckSum}` | cookie-optional | ANON | 2 | public-read | Downloads an asset's file by moderation-agnostic hash and checksum (v2). Heavily used for public assets, but with Roxy's cookie it would also hand out private or copylocked assets the account is allowed to access, so forward without the cookie. *Note:* Permissions leak: with the cookie, Roblox serves any asset Roxy's account can access (its own or group-owned private models, audio, packages, copylocked places it can edit, assets shared to it through asset permissions). Strip the cookie and any caller-sent Roblox-Place-Id/permissionContext style headers should not grant extra access. If Roblox starts refusing anonymous downloads (401), only flip to ALLOW_TOKEN after confirming the account owns, edits and is shared on nothing private. Responses are redirects/locations to signed CDN URLs that expire; cache per asset id plus version for a short time only. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#assetdelivery_get_v2_marAssetHash__marAssetHash__marCheckSum__marCheckSum_) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/assetdelivery#assetdelivery_get_v2_marAssetHash__marAssetHash__marCheckSum__marCheckSum_) |

## badges.roblox.com

| Method | Path | Roblox sign-in | Verdict | Risk | Category | What it does and why | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/v1/badges/metadata` | none | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Returns badge creation price and field length limits. Global reference data. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/badges#badges_get_v1_badges_metadata) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_get_v1_badges_metadata) |
| GET | `/v1/badges/{badgeId}` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Returns public info and award statistics for one badge. | PATCH | `/v1/badges/{badgeId}` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | creator-management | Updates a badge's name, description or enabled state. Would act with whatever manage rights Roxy's account has on the badge's game. *Note:* Needs CSRF token. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/badges#badges_patch_v1_badges__badgeId_) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_patch_v1_badges__badgeId_) |
| POST | `/v1/badges/{badgeId}/icon` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | creator-management | Uploads a new icon over an existing badge. Write acting with the account's permissions, plus an upload abuse vector. *Note:* Needs CSRF token. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#badges_post_v1_badges__badgeId__icon) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_post_v1_badges__badgeId__icon) |
| GET | `/v1/universes/{universeId}/badges` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Lists the badges of a game. Public data, heavily used. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/badges#badges_get_v1_universes__universeId__badges) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_get_v1_universes__universeId__badges) |
| POST | `/v1/universes/{universeId}/badges` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 4 | creator-management | Creates a new badge in a universe and charges the selected payment source (Robux). Spends money and acts with the account's creator permissions. *Note:* Also economy-spend. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/badges#badges_post_v1_universes__universeId__badges) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_post_v1_universes__universeId__badges) |
| GET | `/v1/universes/{universeId}/free-badges-quota` | none | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Returns how many free badges a universe can still create today. Per-universe number with no account data. *Note:* Mainly useful to creators; auth is none so the cookie adds nothing sensitive. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/badges#badges_get_v1_universes__universeId__free_badges_quota) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_get_v1_universes__universeId__free_badges_quota) |
| DELETE | `/v1/user/badges/{badgeId}` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | inventory-private | Removes an awarded badge from the authenticated user. Irreversible write on Roxy's account. *Note:* Needs CSRF token. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/badges#badges_delete_v1_user_badges__badgeId_) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/badges#badges_delete_v1_user_badges__badgeId_) |
| GET | `/v1/users/{userId}/badges` | cookie-required | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Lists badges a given user has earned, respecting that user's inventory privacy. | GET | `/v1/users/{userId}/badges/awarded-dates` | cookie-required | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Batch lookup of when a given user earned specific badges. | GET | `/v1/users/{userId}/badges/{badgeId}/awarded-date` | cookie-required | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Returns when a given user earned one badge (204 if not). | GET | `/v1/assets/{assetId}/bundles` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 2 | public-read | Lists bundles containing an asset. | GET | `/v1/bundles/details` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 2 | public-read | Returns details for a list of bundle ids. Heavily used public read. *Note:* Bundle item entries carry an 'owned' flag computed for the viewer, so with the cookie they reveal which of these items Roxy's account owns. Judged trivial for a throwaway account and outweighed by better rate limits; switch to ALLOW_ANON if the owner wants zero ownership leak. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#catalog_get_v1_bundles_details) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/catalog#catalog_get_v1_bundles_details) |
| GET | `/v1/bundles/{bundleId}/details` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 2 | public-read | Returns details for one bundle. Heavily used public read. *Note:* Bundle item entries carry an 'owned' flag computed for the viewer, so with the cookie they reveal which of these items Roxy's account owns. Judged trivial for a throwaway account and outweighed by better rate limits; switch to ALLOW_ANON if the owner wants zero ownership leak. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#catalog_get_v1_bundles__bundleId__details) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/catalog#catalog_get_v1_bundles__bundleId__details) |
| GET | `/v1/bundles/{bundleId}/recommendations` | cookie-optional | ANON | 2 | public-read | Returns recommended bundles for a bundle (random bundles for id 0). Works anonymously; with the cookie results may be personalized to Roxy's account and carry its 'owned' flags. *Note:* Personalized output would also poison the shared cache. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#catalog_get_v1_bundles__bundleId__recommendations) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/catalog#catalog_get_v1_bundles__bundleId__recommendations) |
| POST | `/v1/catalog/items/details` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 2 | public-read | Batch read of catalog item details (POST is only a read). Judged trivial for a throwaway account and outweighed by better rate limits; switch to ALLOW_ANON if the owner wants zero ownership leak. Needs CSRF token even anonymously. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#catalog_post_v1_catalog_items_details) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/catalog#catalog_post_v1_catalog_items_details) |
| POST | `/v1/catalog/items/details/complete` | cookie-optional | ANON | 2 | public-read | Batch item details 'complete with user ownership and economy information': adds per-viewer owned, isPurchasable and expiration fields. The plain /v1/catalog/items/details covers the public fields. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#catalog_post_v1_catalog_items_details_complete) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/catalog#catalog_post_v1_catalog_items_details_complete) |
| GET | `/v1/categories` | none | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Lists catalog category names and ids. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/assets#catalog_get_v1_categories) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/catalog#catalog_get_v1_categories) |

## games.roblox.com

| Method | Path | Roblox sign-in | Verdict | Risk | Category | What it does and why | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/v1/games` | cookie-optional | TOKEN | 1 | public-read | Returns game details (name, creator, playing, visits, price) for universe ids. Core public data that games rely on. *Note:* Has isFavoritedByUser, which reflects Roxy's account. Trivial since favorite writes are blocked, and it is the same for every caller so caching is fine. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/universes#games_get_v1_games) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/games#games_get_v1_games) |

## users.roblox.com

| Method | Path | Roblox sign-in | Verdict | Risk | Category | What it does and why | Docs |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GET | `/v1/birthdate` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | account-private-data | Returns the account's own birthdate, which is private identifying data. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/accounts#users_get_v1_birthdate) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/users#users_get_v1_birthdate) |
| POST | `/v1/birthdate` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | account-settings-write | Changes the account's birthdate. A write to the account's own profile settings. *Note:* Age changes can trigger age verification or lock chat features; CSRF needed. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/accounts#users_post_v1_birthdate) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/users#users_post_v1_birthdate) |
| GET | `/v1/description` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 2 | account-private-data | Returns the authenticated account's own description. Public descriptions of any user are already available through GET /v1/users/{userId}. *Note:* No userId param: always the account's own data. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/accounts#users_get_v1_description) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/users#users_get_v1_description) |
| POST | `/v1/description` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | account-settings-write | Changes the account's profile description. Could be used to post spam or offensive text as the account and get it moderated. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/accounts#users_post_v1_description) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/users#users_post_v1_description) |
| GET | `/v1/display-names/validate` | cookie-optional | ANON | 1 | public-read | Validates a display name for a brand new user (signup helper). Could be BLOCK if the owner wants every signup helper gone. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/users#users_get_v1_display_names_validate) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/users#users_get_v1_display_names_validate) |
| GET | `/v1/gender` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 2 | account-private-data | Returns the account's own gender setting. | POST | `/v1/gender` | cookie-required | **BLOCK** | 3 | account-settings-write | Changes the account's gender setting. | [feature](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/features/accounts#users_post_v1_gender) [domain](https://create.roblox.com/docs/cloud/reference/domains/users#users_post_v1_gender) |
